They function as oxidoreductases that reduce protein disulfide bonds, playing a key role in maintaining cellular redox homeostasis. The CPHC motif serves as the redox-active center where cysteine residues cycle between reduced thiol and oxidized disulfide states during electron transfer reactions. Evaluating other options: (B) describes oxidative folding enzymes like Ero1 or PDI in its oxidative role, which are typically larger and have different sequence context; (C) describes protein phosphatases which have distinct metal-binding motifs (not CPHC); (D) describes ribosomal function, which is unrelated to this sequence.
